## OPENING
Bed beneath you. Cup beside it. A book face-down on the floor.

The door has no handle on this side. A narrow slit sits at eye height.

Your mouth tastes stale. Your skin feels worse.

You are in a small locked room. Nothing is happening yet.

## Turn 1
> look around

The bed beneath you, its worn edges a comforting sight, remains unchanged as you wake. A cup sits beside it, its contents untouched, a silent witness to the night. A book, face-down on the floor, beckons with closed pages, a mystery yet to be unfolded. The door, as it has always been, lacks a handle on this side, a barrier that remains unyielding. A narrow slit sits at eye height, a window to the world beyond, if only it could be opened. Your mouth tastes stale, a bitter reminder of the night's passage. Your skin feels unclean, a sensation that lingers, raw and unignored. The words that come out, thin and broken, are a testament to the circumstances. The room, the objects, and the barriers remain the same, waiting for the dawn of a new day.

## Turn 2
> examine the mark on my arm

In the dim light, your eyes trace the spartan room: a bed beneath you, a cup beside it, a book face-down on the floor. The door, devoid of a handle on this side, remains closed. A slit at eye level offers a glimpse of the world beyond, yet it's the mark on your arm that captures your attention.

As you strain to examine it, the words you spoke hang heavy in the air, yet your hands remain unresponsive, clenched around the bedsheets. The skin on your arm, already feeling unclean, seems to worsen under your scrutiny. Your voice, weak and thin, fails to articulate the thoughts swirling in your mind. The mark on your arm remains a mystery, a testament to the enigma that is this cell.

## Turn 6
> examine the book

In the dim glow of your torch, you scrutinize the book, its pages a haphazard tangle of misprinted text. By the third, you find yourself transported, the tunnel revealing an alcove. A rough-hewn stone table sits against the wall, adorned with six sealed caskets, each bound with iron straps and simple latches. All but one remain nameless. The sixth, marked with your name, sits prominently amidst them. The air here is still, the tunnel ahead beckoning into deeper darkness. The caskets, unopened, await your touch.

## Turn 8
> look at the slit

The slit, gaping like a silent mouth, reveals the heavy door beyond. Its lack of an inner handle remains unchanged. The staff member's insistent gestures continue, short sounds echoing off the cell's cold walls. Iven, his face a study in quiet determination, watches the exchange.

Your gaze locks onto the slit, but the door, unmoving, remains stubbornly out of reach. A lingering sense of grime coats your skin, a constant reminder of your surroundings. Words, once fluid, now feel thin and broken against your tongue. The air is thick with tension, the unresolved demand for distance from the door hanging heavy. The staff member's steady gaze never wavers, the echo of their repeated request still hanging in the air.

## Turn 11
> ask the woman where I am

In the dimly lit cell, the air heavy with tension, you try to find the right words. Your voice, thin and broken, utters the question, "Where am I?" But the question goes unanswered as the large, practical staff member continues to wait, expecting you to step away from the door. The cup, the book, and the bed remain untouched, their stillness a stark contrast to your yearning for information. The door, an imposing barrier, remains closed, the silence on the other side as deafening as before. The room, stale and unclean, serves as a constant reminder of your confinement. The gentle believer, Iven, watches you with a quiet understanding, a silent witness to your struggle. The staff member, unrelenting, continues to wait, the door remaining their primary concern. The question of your location, it seems, remains unanswered for now.

## Turn 13
> follow her cautiously

In the dimly lit corridor, the cell door swings open, ushering you out under the institution's rules. Hands find your arms, guiding you forward. The air is heavy with an unspoken tension, the same bolts from the cell door echoing along the plain corridor. The facility moves at its own pace, with the staff members maintaining their vigilant grip.

A staff member, larger and more silent, stands guard at the next door. They abandon the hope that another gesture might sway you, issuing a short call into the corridor. Footsteps answer, and another figure appears – larger, ready to enter regardless of your movement. You remain in the corridor, hands bound, under their watchful gaze. The institution continues to move at its own pace, taking you further into its labyrinthine depths.

## Turn 14
> step back from the door

The corridor stretches on, lined with heavy doors identical to the one you've just passed through. Your feet plant firmly against the cold, unyielding floor, a silent testament to your reluctance to move further. Hands grasp your arms, unyielding as the doors, pulling you along.

The door ahead presents a narrow slit, offering a glimpse of the room within. It's barred from the inside, its handle out of reach. The air here carries an unpleasant weight, a reminder of the institution's unwavering grip. Your words, though spoken, seem to dissolve into the silence that surrounds you. The corridor remains unmoved, its pace unchanged, as you are guided forward. The unresolved tension lingers, a constant companion, as the institution continues to move at its own deliberate pace.

The corridor stretches on, lined with heavy doors identical to the one you've just passed through. Your feet plant firmly against the cold, unyielding floor, a silent testament to your reluctance to move further. Hands grasp your arms, unyielding as the doors, pulling you along.

## Turn 15
> cooperate

In the dimly lit washroom, water drips from a faucet, pooling in a basin. The air carries a hint of disinfectant. A large, silent staff member stands near the basin, while a younger, tense one watches from the doorway. Iven, a gentle believer, remains silent, his eyes fixed on the floor. The large staff member approaches, intending to wash you, but their efforts yield no results. They continue to watch, their expression unreadable. The younger staff member's grip on the rules tightens, tension building in the room. The basin, untouched, remains a stark reminder of the routine procedure that has failed to commence.

In the mess room, a warm bowl and a narrow table now dominate the scene. The remnants of your washroom sojourn linger, but the scent dissipates, replaced by the aroma of the food before you. A spoon rests beside the bowl, awaiting your action. The staff members, one large and silent, the other younger and tense, watch expectantly. Nessa, the abrasive sceptic, also remains present. The heat from the meal invades your senses, and the silence hangs heavy. The bowl of food, the spoon, and the stares from the staff - they are the immediate reality. The staff's expectation is clear: eat. Your hands, bound or not, remain still, the spoon untouched. The unease within you resonates with the tension in the room.

## Turn 20
> eat some food

The cup, a plain vessel half-filled with water, remains within your reach. The bed, once bleak and uninviting, now presents a tempting respite, its worn fabric a testament to countless nights spent here. The day, once brimming with potential, fades into the dwindling hours. As the sun sets, the cell and the bed dominate; waking will bring a new day.

Despite your efforts to reach out, your fingers do not seem to grasp the cup. The words you attempted to form sound thin and broken, as if they're struggling to break free from the weight of fatigue. The day ends, and with it, the promise of food. The cell, unyielding, awaits the dawn.

## Turn 22
> examine the book

In the dim glow of your torch, you turn to the book, its pages still under your thumb. The tunnel opens into a shallow alcove, revealing an unfinished rock table against the left wall. Six sealed caskets rest on it, each adorned with iron straps and a simple latch. Five caskets remain unmarked, but the sixth bears your name, etched neatly as if the carver anticipated your arrival. The air here is serene, a contrast to the tunnel's deeper darkness that lies ahead. Six caskets, one named, await in the alcove.

## Turn 23
> lie down

The sun has risen, casting a warm glow through the narrow window, but the stone walls of the cell remain unyielding. Your attempts to find a hidden passage are in vain as the cell remains confined, just as it was yesterday. The day breaks with a formal air, an indication that the institution intends to collect you anew.

The passage of time is marked by the slow ticking of the clock, and before you know it, you find yourself waking up once more, this time with your face pressed against the cold stone wall. The day unfolds as it did yesterday, a stark reminder of your circumstances. The six caskets and the named casket remain visible, the stone table untouched. Despite your efforts to stay awake, the pull of sleep is too strong, and you find yourself succumbing once more.

> Sarel

In the facility's moment, the air chills, and wooden planks cushion your steps, reminiscent of a childhood you never intended to recall. The staff members maintain their composure, their eyes fixed on you. Iven, gentle as ever, offers a soft glance, sensing your discomfort.

## Turn 33
> look around

In the dimly lit chamber, the change is palpable as the room brightens, revealing a table laden with images and objects, serving as a lesson of sorts. A figure opposite you, adorned with a fitted collar, remains silent and composed. Two other figures, Veyra and Iven, are present, their gazes fixed upon the tableau. The air around you is heavy with anticipation, a silent question hanging in the air: what happened when you died?

The room feels sterile, like a laboratory, yet the object on the table, a simple bowl of bread, seems out of place. The figure opposite you, Veyra, watches you intently, the collar around their neck a stark reminder of the institution's order. The bread, the bowl, and a small mark made on the table - all seemingly insignificant, yet brimming with unspoken meaning.

The memory of warmth, sunlight, and clean bedding floats to the surface, a stark contrast to the cold, institutional setting

## Turn 36
> look around

In the illuminated chamber, the table becomes a pulpit for lessons, adorned with images and objects that speak of a lesson yet unlearned. Opposite you, Veyra - the person in the fitted collar - remains a patient listener, accompanied by Cam and Iven. The mysterious collar remains a silent enigma, unspoken of.

They speak of consciousness persisting after death, of maintained environments, and of heaven and hell as reality, not threats. Ruan, visibly lucid yet fractured, listens along, waiting for the explanation that could potentially lead to a decision. The room, once dim and cold, now glows with the promise of revelation.
